\displaystyle\leq C\Big\{\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|F_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

where
\varepsilon>0
is a sufficiently small constant.

Due to the smallness of
\varepsilon
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+\|v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big\{\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|F_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(6.23)

For
|\alpha|\geq 1
, applying
\partial_{x}^{\alpha}
to (
6.17
) and multiplying by
\partial_{x}^{\alpha}u_{\infty}
, and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{d}
, then adding them together, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla v_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}^{0},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}^{1},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}).

(6.24)

Noting that

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle=-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla a_{\infty})-\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})-(\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}})\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+C\|\text{div}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}},

(6.25)

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla v_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})

\displaystyle=-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla v_{\infty})-\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})-(\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla v_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+C\|\text{div}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}},

and

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})

\displaystyle=-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\nabla a_{\infty})-g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}})\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}).

The last term on the right-hand side should be handled carefully. It is shown that

\displaystyle-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})

\displaystyle=(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\text{div}v_{\infty})+(\nabla g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})

\displaystyle=-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\partial_{t}a_{\infty})-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}))+(\nabla g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}).

(6.26)

The first term on the right-hand side of (
6.26
) is given by

\displaystyle-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\partial_{t}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}|^{2}dx+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{t}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}|^{2}dx+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

The second term on the right-hand side of (
6.26
) is given by

\displaystyle-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla a_{\infty})-\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty})-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty},\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C\|g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}})+C\|\text{div}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\tilde{v})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}})+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

The third term on the right-hand side of (
6.26
) is given by

\displaystyle(\nabla g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}.

It then follows from above estimates to show that

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\tilde{a}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{a}\|_{L^{2}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}})\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}|^{2}dx+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}})

\displaystyle+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}.

Noting that

\displaystyle(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}^{0},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}^{1},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}^{0}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}^{1}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+C\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Substituting the above estimates into (
6.24
) and using the smallness of
\varepsilon
, we are able to prove that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+\frac{1}{2}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}})\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{a}\|_{L^{2}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}})\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}})

\displaystyle+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Summing the above inequality with respect to
|\alpha|
from
1
to
s
and using (
6.23
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})+\frac{1}{2}\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|\alpha|=0}^{s}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+C\|F_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}.

In terms of the definition in (
6.18
), we prove that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}E_{0}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+C\|F_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}.

(6.27)

In the next section, we consider the dissipation estimates for the density
a_{\infty}
.

For
|\alpha|\geq 1
, applying
\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}
to
\eqref{Mainsystem}_{2}
, then taking inner product with
\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}
on
\mathbb{R}^{d}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\partial_{t}a_{\infty})-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}^{0},\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty})+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}F_{\infty}^{1},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla v_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}).

(6.28)

By
\eqref{Mainsystem}_{1}
and integration by parts, one has

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\quad-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}^{0},\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty})+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}F_{\infty}^{1},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle\quad-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla v_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}).

(6.29)

Noting that

\displaystyle(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla a_{\infty})-\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty})+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+C(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}^{0},\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty})+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}F_{\infty}^{1},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq 2\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}^{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}F_{\infty}^{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}),

and

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla v_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle=(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla v_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle=(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla v_{\infty})-\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}a_{\infty})+(\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla v_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C(\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}})\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq 2\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}).

The remaining term on the right-hand side of (
6.29
) is given by

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle=(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\nabla a_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle=(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\cdot\nabla a_{\infty})-g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}a_{\infty})+(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla a_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\|\nabla g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+C\|g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}})\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Combining the above estimates together, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 6\varepsilon\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}F_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\tilde{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+C\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(6.30)

Taking the summation of
|\alpha|
from
1
to
s
and using the smallness of
\varepsilon
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})+\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\nabla v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla F_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2})+C\|\nabla v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}.

(6.31)

Taking
\beta_{1}\times
(
6.27
)+(
6.31
) with
\beta_{1}
a sufficiently small positive constant, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big\{E_{0}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)+\beta_{1}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}v_{\infty},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty})\Big\}+\frac{\beta_{1}}{2}\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+C\varepsilon\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\|F_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+C\beta_{1}\Big(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\nabla v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla F_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\nabla v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}\Big).

(6.32)

Due to the smallness of
\beta_{1}
and
\varepsilon
, there exists a positive constant
\bar{\kappa}_{0}>0
such that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}E_{0}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)+\bar{\kappa}_{0}D_{0}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+C\|F_{\infty}^{1}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}+C\|F_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}.

(6.33)

Let
\kappa_{0}=\max\{\bar{\kappa}_{0},\tilde{\kappa}_{0}\}
. In terms of the definition of
E_{0}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)
and
D_{0}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)
, we get (
6.21
). This completes the proof of this proposition.

\hfill\square

In the next content, we aim to establish the estimates for
E_{\ell}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)
and
D_{\ell}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)
with
\ell\geq 1
. Let us introduce a cut-off function. We fix a nonincreasing function
\tilde{\eta}\in C([0,\infty))
satisfying
0\leq\tilde{\eta}\leq 1
and

\displaystyle\tilde{\eta}=\left\{\begin{aligned} &1&&(|r|\leq 1),\\
&0&&(|r|>2).\end{aligned}\right.

Define
\eta_{R}\in C_{0}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
by
\eta_{R}(x)=\tilde{\eta}(\frac{|x|}{R})
. Observe that, for any multi-index
\alpha
with
|\alpha|\geq 1
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mbox{\rm supp}\,(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\eta_{R})\subset\{R\leq|x|\leq 2R\},\quad\Big||x|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}(x))\Big|\leq C_{\alpha}R^{1-|\alpha|},~(x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}).

We first prove the following proposition

Proposition 6.6
.

Let
d\geq 3
and
s
be a nonnegative integer satisfying
s\geq[\frac{d}{2}]+2
and let
\ell
be an integer satisfying
\ell\geq 1
. Assume that

\displaystyle u_{0\infty}={}^{\top}{(a_{0\infty},v_{0\infty})}\in H^{s},~F_{\infty}={}^{\top}{(F_{\infty}^{0},F_{\infty}^{1})}\in L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H^{s})\cap C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s-1}).

Here
T^{\prime}
is a given positive number. Assume also that
u_{\infty}={}^{\top}{(a_{\infty},v_{\infty})}
is the solution of system (
6.17
). If
\nabla\tilde{\phi}\in C([0,T];H^{s-1})\cap L^{2}(0,T;H^{s-1})
,
\tilde{v}\in C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s})\cap L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H^{s})
,
\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\in C([0,T];H^{s-1})
, and that
u_{\infty}
satisfies

\displaystyle a_{\infty}\in C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s}),v_{\infty}\in C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s})\cap L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H^{s}),

Then there exists a positive constant
\kappa_{1}>0
such that the estimate

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}E_{\ell}^{s}[\eta_{R}u_{\infty}](t)+\kappa_{1}D_{\ell}^{s}[\eta_{R}u_{\infty}](t)

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\eta_{R}u_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2},

\displaystyle+C|\eta_{R}u_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2},

(6.34)

holds on
(0,T^{\prime})
. Here
C
is a positive constant independent of
T^{\prime}
, and
R\geq 1
; and
N_{R}
denotes the set
N_{R}=\{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d};R\leq|x|\leq 2R\}
.

Proof.

By multiplying
\eta_{R}
to (
6.17
), we have

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})+\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})+\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})=\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0}+K_{1},\\
&\partial_{t}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})+\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})+\eta_{R}v_{\infty}+\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})+g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})=\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}+K_{2},\end{aligned}\right.

(6.35)

where the nonlinear terms
K_{1}
and
K_{2}
are given by

\displaystyle K_{1}=\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty}+v_{\infty}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R},

\displaystyle K_{2}=a_{\infty}\nabla\eta_{R}+\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R}v_{\infty}+g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty}.

(6.36)

Multiplying (
6.35
) by
|x|^{2\ell}\eta_{R}u_{\infty}
, and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{d}
by parts then adding them together, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\big\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+\big\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\nabla(|x|^{2\ell})\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|x|^{2\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty}+\nabla\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\cdot|x|^{2\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty}+\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R}v_{\infty}+g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|x|^{2\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|x|^{2\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|x|^{2\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0}|x|^{2\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}+\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}|x|^{2\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle:=I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}+I_{4}+I_{5}+I_{6}+I_{7}.

It is shown that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\||x|^{\ell-1}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\||x|^{\ell-1}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{v}\cdot|x|^{2\ell}\nabla(|\eta_{R}a_{\infty}|^{2})\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\text{div}\tilde{v}|x|^{2\ell}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})^{2}\mathrm{d}x+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(|x|^{2\ell})|\eta_{R}a_{\infty}|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C\|\text{div}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell-1}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\||x|^{\ell-1}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\|\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}(N_{R})}\|\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\eta_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}(N_{R})})\|v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}^{2}+C\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}\|v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|u_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq(\|\nabla\eta_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}(N_{R})}\|\nabla\eta_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}(N_{R})}\|v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}

\displaystyle+\|g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}(N_{R})}\|\nabla\eta_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})})\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})})(\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})})\|u_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{v}\cdot|x|^{2\ell}\nabla(|(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|^{2})\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\text{div}\tilde{v}|x|^{2\ell}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})^{2}\mathrm{d}x+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(|x|^{2\ell})|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C\|\text{div}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell-1}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\||x|^{\ell-1}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle\leq\|g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}}\||x|^{\ell}\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\||x|^{\ell}\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}\||x|^{\ell}\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{7}

\displaystyle\leq\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0}\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}+\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon(\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+C\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon(\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+C\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}F_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Combining the estimates of
I_{1}\sim I_{7}
together, one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\big\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+\big\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\varepsilon\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}u_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\||x|^{\ell-1}\eta_{R}u_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}\||x|^{\ell}\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|u_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}F_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(6.37)

It then follows from above and the smallness of
\varepsilon
to prove that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\big\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+\big\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}u_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\||x|^{\ell-1}\eta_{R}u_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}\||x|^{\ell}\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|u_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}(N_{R})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\||x|^{\ell}\eta_{R}F_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(6.38)

For a multi-index
\alpha
satisfying
1\leq|\alpha|\leq s
, we take the inner product of
\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\eqref{Mainsystem1}_{1}
with
|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})
to prove

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(\text{div}\tilde{v},|x|^{2\ell}|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|^{2})-([\partial_{x}^{\alpha},\tilde{v}]\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(|x|^{2\ell}),|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|^{2})+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}K_{1},|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle:=J_{1}^{\alpha}+J_{2}^{\alpha}+J_{3}^{\alpha}+J_{4}^{\alpha}+J_{5}^{\alpha},

(6.39)

where we have used the fact that

\displaystyle(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))+([\partial_{x}^{\alpha},\tilde{v}]\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(|x|^{2\ell}\tilde{v},\nabla|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|^{2})+([\partial_{x}^{\alpha},\tilde{v}]\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}(\text{div}\tilde{v},|x|^{2\ell}|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|^{2})-\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(|x|^{2\ell}),|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|^{2})

\displaystyle~~+([\partial_{x}^{\alpha},\tilde{v}]\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})).

This calculation can be justified by using the standard Friedrichs commutator argument.

Then we estimate
J_{1}^{\alpha}\sim J_{4}^{\alpha}
as follows,

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}J_{1}^{\alpha}\leq C\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\|\text{div}\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2},

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}J_{2}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2},

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}J_{3}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\|\tilde{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell-1}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}+C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2},

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}J_{4}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty}+v_{\infty}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|\nabla\eta_{R}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|\nabla\eta_{R}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})},

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}J_{5}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0})\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}.

Summing
|\alpha|
from
1
to
s
, we are able to show that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}(\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2})+C\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}.

(6.40)

We take the inner product of
\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\eqref{Mainsystem1}_{2}
with
|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})
and integrate by parts to produce

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))+\big\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),\nabla(|x|^{2\ell})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}K_{2},|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle:=J_{6}^{\alpha}+J_{7}^{\alpha}+J_{8}^{\alpha}+J_{9}^{\alpha}+J_{10}^{\alpha}.

(6.41)

It is directly proved that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}J_{6}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\||x|^{\ell-1}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}.

For
J_{7}^{\alpha}
, we consider it below,

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}J_{7}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty}+\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R}v_{\infty}+g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\eta_{R}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|\nabla\eta_{R}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}

\displaystyle+\|g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|\nabla\eta_{R}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}.

For
J_{8}^{\alpha}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}J_{8}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))-\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))-(\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))-\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\quad+(\mbox{\rm div}\,\tilde{v},|x|^{2\ell}|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|^{2})+(\tilde{v},\nabla(|x|^{2\ell})|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|^{2})

\displaystyle\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}.

For
J_{9}^{\alpha}
, we have

\displaystyle J_{9}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))-g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle:=J_{91}^{\alpha}+J_{92}^{\alpha}.

The first term
J_{91}^{\alpha}
is estimated as follows,

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}J_{91}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}.

(6.42)

In terms of integration by parts, we have

\displaystyle J_{92}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=(\nabla g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))+(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),\nabla(|x|^{2\ell})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle+(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})).

(6.43)

It is shown that

\displaystyle(\nabla g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))+(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),\nabla(|x|^{2\ell})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~+C\|g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell-1}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell-1}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\||x|^{\ell-1}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}((\nabla g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))+(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),\nabla(|x|^{2\ell})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})))

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}.

The remaining term on the right-hand side of (
6.43
) is given by

\displaystyle(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|x|^{2\ell},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\partial_{t}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|x|^{2\ell},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})))

\displaystyle+(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty}+v_{\infty}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R})).

(6.44)

Noting that

\displaystyle-\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\partial_{t}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\big||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\big|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{t}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\big||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\big|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\big||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\big|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2},

and

\displaystyle-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|x|^{2\ell},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|x|^{2\ell},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))-\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|x|^{2\ell},\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})).

Then we have

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|x|^{2\ell},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))-\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})))

\displaystyle\leq C\|g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}((g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|x|^{2\ell},\tilde{v}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=-\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\mbox{\rm div}\,((g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\tilde{v})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\eta_{R}a_{\infty})-\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}((g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\tilde{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),\nabla(|x|^{2\ell})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\eta_{R}a_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s-1}}\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2})|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}.

It then concludes from above to show that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(-(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|x|^{2\ell},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}.

The third term on the right-hand side of (
6.44
) is estimates as

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty}+v_{\infty}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R}))

\displaystyle\leq C\|g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}(N_{R})}\|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}\|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2})(\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}).

Therefore we conclude from above that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}J_{92}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\big||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\big|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2})(\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}),

which together with (
6.42
) yields that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}J_{9}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\big||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\big|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2})(\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}).

The last term
J_{10}^{\alpha}
is estimated as follows,

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}J_{10}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1})\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}.

Summing
|\alpha|
from
1
to
s
and noting the smallness of
\varepsilon
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}-\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\big|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\big|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\big||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\big|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C|\nabla(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}.

(6.45)

Combining the estimates (
6.45
) and (
6.40
) together, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\big|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+\big|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\big|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}\big)+\frac{1}{2}\big|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\big||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\big|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C|\nabla(\eta_{R}F_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}+C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2},

which together with (
6.38
) yields that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\big|\eta_{R}a_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+\big|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\big|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}\big)+\big|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|=0}^{s}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\big||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\big|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}a_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s}}^{2}+C\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\eta_{R}a_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}.

(6.46)

We next consider the estimates of
\big\||x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a_{\infty}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
for
1\leq|\alpha|\leq s
. For multi-index
\alpha
satisfying
1\leq|\alpha|\leq s
, we take the inner product of
\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}\eqref{Mainsystem1}_{3}

with
|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})
to prove

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L_{\ell}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}K_{2},|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})).

Note

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\partial_{t}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}\partial_{t}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle~~+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),\nabla(|x|^{2\ell})\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}\partial_{t}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})),

and

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})=-\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})-\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})+\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0}+K_{1}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L_{\ell}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})+\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})-K_{1})

\displaystyle+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),\nabla(|x|^{2\ell})\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})+\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})-\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}-K_{1})

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}K_{2},|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle-(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))+(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle:=L_{1}^{\alpha}+L_{2}^{\alpha}+L_{3}^{\alpha}+L_{4}^{\alpha}+L_{5}^{\alpha}+L_{6}^{\alpha}+L_{7}^{\alpha}+L_{8}^{\alpha}.

It is easily seen that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}L_{1}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})))

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle-\sum_{|\alpha=1|^{s}}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}K_{1})

\displaystyle\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C(1+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}.

The second term
L_{2}^{\alpha}
is given by

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}L_{2}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),\nabla(|x|^{2\ell})\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})+\text{div}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})-\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}-K_{1}))

\displaystyle\leq C|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}+C|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}

\displaystyle+C|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}+C\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}(\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})

\displaystyle\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(1+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}.

The third term
L_{3}^{\alpha}
is given by

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}L_{3}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\||x|^{\ell-1}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}\||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}.

The fourth term
L_{4}^{\alpha}
is given by

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}L_{4}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{1}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}.

The fifth term
L_{5}^{\alpha}
is given by

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}L_{5}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}K_{2},|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle=\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(a_{\infty}\nabla\eta_{R}+\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla\eta_{R}v_{\infty}+g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\nabla\eta_{R}a_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\leq C(\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}\|v_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|a_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}.

The sixth term
L_{6}^{\alpha}
is given by

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}L_{6}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\tilde{v}\cdot\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\leq C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}.

The seventh term
L_{7}^{\alpha}
is given by

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}L_{7}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}.

The last term
L_{8}^{\alpha}
is given by

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}L_{8}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}))

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}^{0}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}.

It then concludes from above that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))+|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\varepsilon|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}.

Using the smallness of
\varepsilon
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|=1}^{s}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))+\frac{1}{2}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}.

(6.47)

Taking
\beta_{2}\times\eqref{101003}+\eqref{110101}
with
\beta_{2}
a small positive constant, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big\{\big|\eta_{R}a_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+\big|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}\big|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+\beta_{2}(\partial_{x}^{\alpha-1}(\eta_{R}v_{\infty}),|x|^{2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty}))\Big\}+\big|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|=0}^{s}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{3}(\tilde{\phi})\big||x|^{\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})\big|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{3}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\eta_{R}a_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C|\eta_{R}a_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s}}^{2}+C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+\beta_{2}\Big\{C(\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\nabla(\eta_{R}a_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}v_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C|\nabla(\eta_{R}v_{\infty})|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon|\eta_{R}a_{\infty}|_{L^{2}_{\ell}}^{2}.

Since
\beta_{2}
and
\varepsilon
are small positive constants, we deduce that there exists a positive constant
\bar{\kappa}_{1}
such that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}E_{\ell}^{s}[\eta_{R}u_{\infty}](t)+\bar{\kappa}_{1}D_{\ell}^{s}[\eta_{R}u_{\infty}](t)

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon|\eta_{R}a_{\infty}|_{L^{2}_{\ell}}^{2}+C(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\eta_{R}u_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C|\eta_{R}u_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}.

(6.48)

Similarly, there exists a positive constant
\tilde{\kappa}_{1}
such that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}E_{\ell}^{s-1}[\eta_{R}u_{\infty}](t)+\tilde{\kappa}_{1}D_{\ell}^{s-1}[\eta_{R}u_{\infty}](t)

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon|\eta_{R}a_{\infty}|_{L^{2}_{\ell}}^{2}+C(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|\eta_{R}u_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C|\eta_{R}u_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s-1}}^{2}+C|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}.

(6.49)

Let
\kappa_{1}=\max\{\bar{\kappa}_{1},\tilde{\kappa}_{1}\}
. We combine (
6.48
) and (
6.49
) together to complete the proof of the proposition.

\hfill\square

Remark 6.7
.

It should be noted that the above proposition also holds with
\eta_{R}
and
N_{R}
replaced by
\eta_{R}-\eta_{R}^{\prime}
and
N_{R,R^{\prime}}
for
R^{\prime}>R\geq 1
, where
N_{R,R^{\prime}}
denoted the set
N_{R,R^{\prime}}=\{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d};R\leq|x|\leq 2R^{\prime}\}
.

Proposition 6.8
.

Let
d\geq 3
and
s
be a nonnegative integer satisfying
s\geq[\frac{d}{2}]+2
and let
\ell
be an integer satisfying
\ell\geq 1
. Assume that

\displaystyle u_{0\infty}={}^{\top}{(a_{0\infty},v_{0\infty})}\in H^{s},~F_{\infty}={}^{\top}{(F_{\infty}^{0},F_{\infty}^{1})}\in L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H^{s})\cap C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s-1}).

Here
T^{\prime}
is a given positive number. Assume also that
u_{\infty}={}^{\top}{(a_{\infty},v_{\infty})}
is the solution of system (
6.17
). If
\nabla\tilde{\phi}\in C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s-1})\cap L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H^{s-1}),\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\in C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s-1})
,
\tilde{v}\in C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s})\cap L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H^{s})
and that
u_{\infty}
satisfies

\displaystyle a_{\infty}\in C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s}),~v_{\infty}\in C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s})\cap L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H^{s}),

Then there exists a positive constant
\kappa_{2}
such that the estimate

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}E_{\ell}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)+\kappa_{2}D_{\ell}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\|a_{\infty}\|_{L^{2}_{\ell}}^{2}+C(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})|u_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C|u_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell-1}^{s}}^{2}+C|F_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}

(6.50)

holds on
(0,T^{\prime})
, where
C>0
is a constant independent of
T^{\prime}
.

Proof.

To complete the proof of this proposition, we apply the induction method to prove

\displaystyle\eta_{R}u_{\infty}\rightarrow u_{\infty}~\text{in}~C([0,T^{\prime}];H_{\ell}^{s})\cap L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H_{\ell}^{s})

as
R\rightarrow{\infty}
.
It is easy to observe that

\displaystyle\eta_{R}u_{\infty}\rightarrow u_{\infty}~\text{in}~C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s})\cap L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H^{s}),

(6.51)

and

\displaystyle\mbox{\rm supp}\,(\eta_{R}-\eta_{R^{\prime}})\subset N_{R,R^{\prime}}=\{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d};R\leq|x|\leq 2R^{\prime}\}~\text{for}~R^{\prime}>R.

Thus it holds

\displaystyle|\eta_{R}u_{\infty}-\eta_{R^{\prime}}u_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}\leq C|u_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}(N_{R,R^{\prime}})}.

Let

\displaystyle\varphi_{\ell,R,R^{\prime}}(t)

\displaystyle=|\eta_{R}u_{\infty}(t)-\eta_{R^{\prime}}u_{\infty}(t)|_{H_{\ell}^{s}},

\displaystyle b(t)

\displaystyle=1+\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}(t)\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}(t)\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}(t)\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}(t)\|_{H^{s}}^{2}\in L^{1}(0,T^{\prime}),

\displaystyle a_{\ell,R,R^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=|\eta_{R}u_{0\infty}-\eta_{R^{\prime}}u_{0\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|\eta_{R}F_{\infty}-\eta_{R^{\prime}}F_{\infty}|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}(1+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}(\tau)\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}(\tau)\|_{H^{s-1}(N_{R})}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\tilde{v}(\tau)\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}+\|\tilde{v}(\tau)\|_{H^{s}(N_{R})}^{2})\|u_{\infty}(\tau)\|_{H^{s}(N_{R,R^{\prime}})}\mathrm{d}\tau.

Then for
\ell=1
, we have

\displaystyle\varphi_{1,R,R^{\prime}}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}D_{1}^{s}[\eta_{R}u_{\infty}-\eta_{R^{\prime}}u_{\infty}]\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\Big\{a_{1,R,R^{\prime}}(T^{\prime})+\int_{0}^{t}b(\tau)\varphi_{1,R,R^{\prime}}(\tau)\mathrm{d}\tau\Big\}.

(6.52)

By Grönwall inequality, we have

\displaystyle\varphi_{1,R,R^{\prime}}(t)\leq Ca_{1,R,R^{\prime}}(T^{\prime})e^{C\int_{0}^{T^{\prime}}b(\tau)\mathrm{d}\tau}

for
t\in[0,T^{\prime}]
. Since
a_{1,R,R^{\prime}}(t)\rightarrow{0}

as

R,R^{\prime}\rightarrow{\infty}
, we see that

\underset{0\leq t\leq T^{\prime}}{\sup}\varphi_{1,R,R^{\prime}}(t)\rightarrow{0}

as

R,R^{\prime}\rightarrow{\infty}.
This, together with (
6.52
), yields that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T^{\prime}}D_{1}^{s}[\eta_{R}u_{\infty}-\eta_{R^{\prime}}u_{\infty}]\mathrm{d}\tau\rightarrow 0~\text{as}~R,R^{\prime}\rightarrow{\infty}.

In view of (
6.51
), we conclude that
\{\eta_{R}u_{\infty}\}
is a Cauchy sequence in
C([0,T^{\prime}];H_{1}^{s})\cap L^{2}([0,T^{\prime}];H_{1}^{s}\times H_{1}^{s})
and

\displaystyle\eta_{R}u_{\infty}\rightarrow{u_{\infty}}~\text{in}~C([0,T^{\prime}];H_{1}^{s})\cap L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H_{1}^{s}),

\text{as}~R\rightarrow{\infty}
. Let
R\rightarrow{\infty}
in with
\ell=1
, we have the desired estimate (
6.50
) in Proposition
6.8
with
\ell=1
.

We next suppose that Proposition
6.8
holds for
\ell=m
. We will prove that it also holds for
\ell=m+1
. By (
6.34
) and Remark
6.7
, we have

\displaystyle\varphi_{m+1,R,R^{\prime}}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}D_{m+1}^{s}[\eta_{R}u_{\infty}-\eta_{R^{\prime}}u_{\infty}]\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C\Big\{a_{m+1,R,R^{\prime}}(T^{\prime})+\int_{0}^{t}b(\tau)\varphi_{m+1,R,R^{\prime}}(\tau)\mathrm{d}\tau\Big\}.

(6.53)

In terms of Gr
\ddot{\text{o}}
nwall inequality, it holds

\displaystyle\varphi_{m+1,R,R^{\prime}}(t)\leq Ca_{m+1,R,R^{\prime}}(T^{\prime})e^{C\int_{0}^{T^{\prime}}b(\tau)\mathrm{d}\tau}.

(6.54)

By the induction assumption, we see that

\displaystyle a_{m+1,R,R^{\prime}}(T^{\prime})\rightarrow{0}~\text{as}~R,R^{\prime}\rightarrow{\infty},

and hence, by (
6.54
) we have

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\varphi_{m+1,R,R^{\prime}}(t)\rightarrow{0},~\text{as}~R,R^{\prime}\rightarrow{\infty}.

It then follows that

\eta_{R}u_{\infty}
is a Cauchy sequence in
C([0,T^{\prime}];H_{m+1}^{s})\cap L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H_{m+1}^{s})
and

\displaystyle\eta_{R}u_{\infty}\rightarrow{u_{\infty}}~\text{in}~C([0,T^{\prime}];H_{m+1}^{s})\cap L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H_{m+1}^{s}),

as
R\rightarrow{\infty}
. Denote the right-hand side of (
6.34
) by
G_{\ell}^{s}(t)
. Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}E_{\ell}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)=G_{\ell}^{s}(t)

on
(0,T^{\prime})
for some
G_{\ell}^{s}(t)\in L^{1}(0,T^{\prime})
. Thus
E_{\ell}^{s}[U_{\infty}](t)
is absolutely continuous in
t\in[0,T^{\prime}]
. Let
R\rightarrow{\infty}
, we have the desired estimate in Proposition
6.8
with
\ell=m+1
.
This completes the proof.

\hfill\square

In view of Proposition
6.4
,

S_{{\infty},\tilde{u}}(t)

(t\geq 0)

and
{\mbox{\sy S}\,}_{\infty,\tilde{u}}(t)

(t\in[0,T])
are defined as follows.

We fix an integer
s
satisfying
s\geq[\frac{d}{2}]+2

and a function
\tilde{u}={}^{\top}{(\tilde{a},\tilde{v})}
satisfying

\displaystyle\nabla\tilde{\phi}\in C_{per}(\mathbb{R};H^{s-1})\cap L^{2}_{per}(\mathbb{R};H^{s-1}),\quad\tilde{v}\in C_{per}(\mathbb{R};H^{s})\cap L^{2}_{per}(\mathbb{R};H^{s}).

(6.55)

The operator
S_{{\infty},\tilde{u}}(t):H^{s}_{(\infty)}\,\longrightarrow\,H^{s}_{(\infty)}

(t\geq 0)

is defined by

u_{\infty}(t)=S_{{\infty},\tilde{u}}(t)u_{0\infty}\ \ \mbox{\rm for}\ \ u_{0\infty}={}^{\top}(a_{0\infty},v_{0\infty})\in H^{s}_{(\infty)},

where
u_{\infty}(t)
is the solution of
(\ref{eq:(2)})

with
F_{\infty}=0
;
and the operator

{\mbox{\sy S}\,}_{\infty,\tilde{u}}(t):L^{2}(0,T;H^{s}_{(\infty)}\times H^{s}_{(\infty)})\,\longrightarrow\,H^{s}_{(\infty)}

(t\in[0,T])
is defined by

\displaystyle u_{\infty}(t)={\mbox{\sy S}\,}_{\infty,\tilde{u}}(t)[F_{\infty}]\ \ \mbox{\rm for}\ \ F_{\infty}={}^{\top}{(F_{\infty}^{0},F_{\infty}^{1})}\in L^{2}(0,T;H^{s}_{(\infty)})\cap C([0,T^{\prime}];H_{(\infty)}^{s-1}),

where
u_{\infty}(t)
is the solution of
(\ref{eq:(2)})
with
u_{0\infty}=0
.

Proposition 6.9
.

Let
d\geq 3
and
s
be an integer satisfying
s\geq[\frac{d}{2}]+2.

Assume that

\displaystyle u_{0\infty}={}^{\top}{(a_{0\infty},v_{0\infty})}\in H^{s}_{(\infty),\ell},

\displaystyle F_{\infty}={}^{\top}{(F_{\infty}^{0},F_{\infty}^{1})}\in L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H^{s}_{(\infty),\ell})\cap C([0,T^{\prime}];H_{(\infty),\ell}^{s-1}).

(6.56)

for all
T^{\prime}>0
and
\tilde{u}={}^{\top}{(\tilde{a},\tilde{v})}
satisfies (
6.55
). Assume also that
u_{\infty}={}^{\top}{(a_{\infty},v_{\infty})}
is the solution of (
4
) satisfying

\displaystyle a_{\infty}\in C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s}_{(\infty)}),v_{\infty}\in C([0,T^{\prime}];H^{s}_{(\infty)})\cap L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H^{s}_{(\infty)})

(6.57)

for all
T^{\prime}>0
.

Then there exist a positive constant
\delta
and an energy function
\mathcal{E}^{s}[u_{\infty}]
such that if

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{C([0,T];H^{s-1})}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{C([0,T];H^{s-1})}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{C([0,T];H^{s})}\leq\delta,

there holds the estimate

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)+\kappa(\|\nabla a_{\infty}(t)\|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}+\|v_{\infty}(t)\|_{H_{\ell}^{s}})

\displaystyle\leq C\Big\{(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})\|u_{\infty}\|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+\|F_{\infty}\|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}\Big\},

(6.58)

on
(0,T^{\prime})
for all
T^{\prime}>0
. Here
\kappa
is a positive constant; C is a positive constant depending of
T
bu not no
T^{\prime}
;
\mathcal{E}^{s}[u_{\infty}]
is equivalent to
\|u_{\infty}\|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}
, i.e.,

\displaystyle C^{-1}\|u_{\infty}\|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}\leq\mathcal{E}^{s}[u_{\infty}]\leq C\|u_{\infty}\|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2},

and

\mathcal{E}^{s}[u_{\infty}](t)
is absolutely continuous in
t\in[0,T^{\prime}]
for all
T^{\prime}>0
.

Proof.

Let
U={}^{\top}{(\Phi,V)}
with
{}^{\top}(\nabla\Phi,V)\in C([0,T^{\prime}];H_{\ell}^{s-1}\times H_{\ell}^{s})\cap L^{2}([0,T^{\prime}];H_{\ell}^{s-1}\times H_{\ell}^{s})
. Then by Lemma
4.2
, we see that

\displaystyle\|P_{1}B[\tilde{u}]U\|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}\leq C\delta\|{}^{\top}(\nabla\Phi,V)\|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}\times H_{\ell}^{s}}.

It then follows from the Propositions
6.5
and
6.8
that there exists a unique solution
U_{\infty}\in C([0,T^{\prime}];H_{\ell}^{s}\times H_{\ell}^{s})\cap L^{2}(0,T^{\prime};H_{\ell}^{s}\times H_{\ell}^{s})
of

\displaystyle\partial_{t}U_{\infty}+AU_{\infty}+B[\tilde{u}]U_{\infty}=F_{\infty}+P_{1}\big(B[\tilde{u}]U\big),\quad U_{\infty}|_{t=0}=u_{0\infty},

(6.59)

and
U_{\infty}
satisfies

\displaystyle\|U_{\infty}(t)\|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+\kappa\int_{0}^{t}(\|\nabla\Phi_{\infty}(\tau)\|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}}^{2}+\|V_{\infty}\|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2})d\tau

\displaystyle\leq C\Big\{\|u_{0\infty}\|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|F_{\infty}\|_{H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}d\tau

\displaystyle+\delta^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|{}^{\top}(\nabla\Phi,V)\|_{H_{\ell}^{s-1}\times H_{\ell}^{s}}^{2}d\tau+\int_{0}^{t}b(\tau)\|U_{\infty}\|_{H^{s}}^{2}d\tau\Big\}.

(6.60)

Here
b(\tau)=1+(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}+\|\nabla\tilde{\phi}\|_{H^{s-1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\tilde{v}\|_{H^{s}}^{2})
.

We set
U_{\infty}^{(0)}=0
and define
U_{\infty}^{(n)},(n=1,2,...)
inductively by the solution of (
6.59
) with
U=U_{\infty}^{(n-1)}
. Applying the Grönwall inequality to (
6.60
) with
U_{\infty}=U_{\infty}^{(1)}
and
U=0
, we have

\displaystyle\|U_{\infty}^{(1)}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\leq M_{4}

for
t\in[0,T^{\prime}]
, where
